This document provides the text form of the November 2026 Advisory Risk Matrices. Please note that the CVE IDs in this document correspond to the same CVE IDs in the  [November 2026 Advisory](https://www.oracle.com/security-alerts/cspunov2026.html).

This page contains the following text format Risk Matrices:


This table provides the text form of the Risk Matrix for Oracle Database Server.


| CVE ID | Description | 
|---|---|
| [CVE-2026-111879] | Security-in-Depth issue in the RDBMS (DBI) component of Oracle Database Server. This vulnerability cannot be exploited in the context of this product. \[ [Advisory](https://www.oracle.com/security-alerts/cspunov2026.html)\] | 
| [CVE-2026-114819] | Vulnerability in the Oracle Text component of Oracle Database Server. Supported versions that are affected are 19.3-19.32, 21.3-21.24 and 23.4.0-23.26.3. Easily exploitable vulnerability allows low privileged attacker having Create Session privilege with network access via Oracle Net to compromise Oracle Text. While the vulnerability is in Oracle Text, attacks may significantly impact additional products (scope change). Successful attacks of this vulnerability can result in takeover of Oracle Text. CVSS 3.1 Base Score 9.9 (Confidentiality, Integrity and Availability impacts). CVSS Vector: (CVSS:3.1/AV:N/AC:L/PR:L/UI:N/S:C/C:H/I:H/A:H). ( [legend](https://www.first.org/cvss/specification-document#i2)) \[[Advisory](https://www.oracle.com/security-alerts/cspunov2026.html)\] | 
| [CVE-2026-114822] | Vulnerability in the RDBMS component of Oracle Database Server. Supported versions that are affected are 23.4.0-23.26.4. Easily exploitable vulnerability allows low privileged attacker having Create Session privilege with network access via Oracle Net to compromise RDBMS. While the vulnerability is in RDBMS, attacks may significantly impact additional products (scope change). Successful attacks of this vulnerability can result in takeover of RDBMS. CVSS 3.1 Base Score 9.9 (Confidentiality, Integrity and Availability impacts). CVSS Vector: (CVSS:3.1/AV:N/AC:L/PR:L/UI:N/S:C/C:H/I:H/A:H). ( [legend](https://www.first.org/cvss/specification-document#i2)) \[[Advisory](https://www.oracle.com/security-alerts/cspunov2026.html)\] | 
| [CVE-2026-114848] | Vulnerability in the Oracle Text component of Oracle Database Server. Supported versions that are affected are 19.3-19.33, 21.3-21.24 and 23.4.0-23.26.3. Easily exploitable vulnerability allows low privileged attacker having Create Session privilege with network access via Oracle Net to compromise Oracle Text. While the vulnerability is in Oracle Text, attacks may significantly impact additional products (scope change). Successful attacks of this vulnerability can result in takeover of Oracle Text. CVSS 3.1 Base Score 9.9 (Confidentiality, Integrity and Availability impacts). CVSS Vector: (CVSS:3.1/AV:N/AC:L/PR:L/UI:N/S:C/C:H/I:H/A:H). ( [legend](https://www.first.org/cvss/specification-document#i2)) \[[Advisory](https://www.oracle.com/security-alerts/cspunov2026.html)\] | 
| [CVE-2026-115557] | Vulnerability in the RDBMS (Python) component of Oracle Database Server. Supported versions that are affected are 21.3-21.24 and 23.4.0-23.26.3. Difficult to exploit vulnerability allows unauthenticated attacker with network access via multiple protocols to compromise RDBMS (Python). Successful attacks of this vulnerability can result in unauthorized creation, deletion or modification access to critical data or all RDBMS (Python) accessible data as well as unauthorized access to critical data or complete access to all RDBMS (Python) accessible data. CVSS 3.1 Base Score 7.4 (Confidentiality and Integrity impacts). CVSS Vector: (CVSS:3.1/AV:N/AC:H/PR:N/UI:N/S:U/C:H/I:H/A:N). ( [legend](https://www.first.org/cvss/specification-document#i2)) \[[Advisory](https://www.oracle.com/security-alerts/cspunov2026.html)\] The patch for CVE-2026-115557 also addresses CVE-2024-3220, CVE-2026-102633, CVE-2026-114583, and CVE-2026-77214. | 
| [CVE-2026-64607] | Vulnerability in the RDBMS (Apache HttpClient) component of Oracle Database Server. Supported versions that are affected are 19.3-19.33, 21.3-21.24 and 23.4.0-23.26.4. Easily exploitable vulnerability allows unauthenticated attacker with network access via HTTP to compromise RDBMS (Apache HttpClient). Successful attacks require human interaction from a person other than the attacker. Successful attacks of this vulnerability can result in unauthorized ability to cause a partial denial of service (partial DOS) of RDBMS (Apache HttpClient). CVSS 3.1 Base Score 4.3 (Availability impacts). CVSS Vector: (CVSS:3.1/AV:N/AC:L/PR:N/UI:R/S:U/C:N/I:N/A:L). ( [legend](https://www.first.org/cvss/specification-document#i2)) \[[Advisory](https://www.oracle.com/security-alerts/cspunov2026.html)\] | 
| [CVE-2026-72897] | Vulnerability in the RDBMS (OpenSSL) component of Oracle Database Server. Supported versions that are affected are 19.32-19.33 and 23.4.0-23.26.4. Easily exploitable vulnerability allows unauthenticated attacker with network access via TLS to compromise RDBMS (OpenSSL). Successful attacks of this vulnerability can result in unauthorized ability to cause a hang or frequently repeatable crash (complete DOS) of RDBMS (OpenSSL). CVSS 3.1 Base Score 7.5 (Availability impacts). CVSS Vector: (CVSS:3.1/AV:N/AC:L/PR:N/UI:N/S:U/C:N/I:N/A:H). ( [legend](https://www.first.org/cvss/specification-document#i2)) \[[Advisory](https://www.oracle.com/security-alerts/cspunov2026.html)\] The patch for CVE-2026-72897 also addresses CVE-2026-35189, CVE-2026-35191, CVE-2026-42772, CVE-2026-54872, CVE-2026-54873, CVE-2026-54875, CVE-2026-75804, CVE-2026-75805, CVE-2026-75806, CVE-2026-77696, CVE-2026-84782, and CVE-2026-84784. | 
| [CVE-2026-75140] | Security-in-Depth issue in the RDBMS (jsoup) component of Oracle Database Server. This vulnerability cannot be exploited in the context of this product. \[ [Advisory](https://www.oracle.com/security-alerts/cspunov2026.html)\] | 
| [CVE-2026-77185] | Security-in-Depth issue in the Oracle SQLcl (Apache MINA SSHD) component of Oracle Database Server. This vulnerability cannot be exploited in the context of this product. \[ [Advisory](https://www.oracle.com/security-alerts/cspunov2026.html)\] | 
| [CVE-2026-77420] | Security-in-Depth issue in the RDBMS (jline) component of Oracle Database Server. This vulnerability cannot be exploited in the context of this product. \[ [Advisory](https://www.oracle.com/security-alerts/cspunov2026.html)\] | 
| [CVE-2026-77421] | Security-in-Depth issue in the RDBMS (jline) component of Oracle Database Server. This vulnerability cannot be exploited in the context of this product. \[ [Advisory](https://www.oracle.com/security-alerts/cspunov2026.html)\] | 
| [CVE-2026-77422] | Security-in-Depth issue in the RDBMS (jline) component of Oracle Database Server. This vulnerability cannot be exploited in the context of this product. \[ [Advisory](https://www.oracle.com/security-alerts/cspunov2026.html)\] | 
| [CVE-2026-89425] | Vulnerability in the Fleet Patching and Provisioning (jackson-core) component of Oracle Database Server. Supported versions that are affected are 19.3-19.33, 21.3-21.24 and 23.4.0-23.26.4. Easily exploitable vulnerability allows low privileged attacker having None privilege with network access via HTTP to compromise Fleet Patching and Provisioning (jackson-core). Successful attacks of this vulnerability can result in unauthorized ability to cause a hang or frequently repeatable crash (complete DOS) of Fleet Patching and Provisioning (jackson-core). CVSS 3.1 Base Score 6.5 (Availability impacts). CVSS Vector: (CVSS:3.1/AV:N/AC:L/PR:L/UI:N/S:U/C:N/I:N/A:H). ( [legend](https://www.first.org/cvss/specification-document#i2)) \[[Advisory](https://www.oracle.com/security-alerts/cspunov2026.html)\] The patch for CVE-2026-89425 also addresses CVE-2026-89407. |

CVSS 3.1 Base Score 7.5 (Availability impacts). CVSS Vector: (CVSS:3.1/AV:N/AC:L/PR:N/UI:N/S:U/C:N/I:N/A:H). Easily exploitable vulnerability allows low privileged attacker with logon to the infrastructure where Oracle Hyperion Data Relationship Management executes to compromise Oracle Hyperion Data Relationship Management. Successful attacks of this vulnerability can result in unauthorized read access to a subset of Oracle Hyperion Data Relationship Management accessible data. CVSS 3.1 Base Score 3.3 (Confidentiality impacts). CVSS Vector: (CVSS:3.1/AV:L/AC:L/PR:L/UI:N/S:U/C:L/I:N/A:N). ( [legend](https://www.first.org/cvss/specification-document#i2)) \[[Advisory](https://www.oracle.com/security-alerts/cspunov2026.html)\] |

CVSS 3.1 Base Score 7.8 (Confidentiality, Integrity and Availability impacts). CVSS Vector: (CVSS:3.1/AV:L/AC:L/PR:N/UI:R/S:U/C:H/I:H/A:H). ( [legend](https://www.first.org/cvss/specification-document#i2)) \[[Advisory](https://www.oracle.com/security-alerts/cspunov2026.html)\] | 
| [CVE-2026-101356] | Vulnerability in the Oracle Agile PLM product of Oracle Supply Chain (component: File Manager (Apache Tomcat)). The supported version that is affected is 9.3.6. Easily exploitable vulnerability allows unauthenticated attacker with network access via HTTP/2 to compromise Oracle Agile PLM. Successful attacks of this vulnerability can result in unauthorized access to critical data or complete access to all Oracle Agile PLM accessible data and unauthorized ability to cause a hang or frequently repeatable crash (complete DOS) of Oracle Agile PLM. CVSS 3.1 Base Score 9.1 (Confidentiality and Availability impacts). CVSS Vector: (CVSS:3.1/AV:N/AC:L/PR:N/UI:N/S:U/C:H/I:N/A:H).
